import fastf1

import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

session = fastf1.get_session(2024, 'Silverstone', 'R')

session.load(telemetry=True)


laps = session.laps.copy()

laps = laps[laps['LapTime'].notna()]

results = session.results[['Abbreviation','TeamName','GridPosition',
'ClassifiedPosition','Status']].copy()

df = laps.merge(results, left_on='Driver', right_on='Abbreviation', how='left')


for col in ['LapTime','Sector1Time','Sector2Time','Sector3Time']:
df[col] = pd.to_timedelta(df[col], errors='coerce').dt.total_seconds()


df = df.dropna(subset=['LapTime'])


tel_rows = []

for i, lap in session.laps.iterrows():
try:
# Rectified line: using pick_drivers and pick_laps as recommended
lap_obj = session.laps.pick_drivers(lap['Driver']).pick_laps(lap['LapNumber']).iloc[0]
tel = lap_obj.get_telemetry()

tel_rows.append({
'Driver': lap['Driver'],
'LapNumber': lap['LapNumber'],
'MaxSpeed': tel['Speed'].max(),
'AvgSpeed': tel['Speed'].mean(),
'AvgThrottle': tel['Throttle'].mean(),
'AvgBrake': tel['Brake'].mean(),
'AvgRPM': tel['RPM'].mean(),
})
except:
continue

tel_df = pd.DataFrame(tel_rows)

if not tel_df.empty:
df = df.merge(tel_df, on=['Driver','LapNumber'], how='left')

if 'AvgSpeed' not in df.columns:
df['AvgSpeed'] = np.nan


weather = session.weather_data.copy()
weather['Time'] = pd.to_timedelta(weather['Time']).dt.total_seconds()

df['LapStartTime_s'] = pd.to_timedelta(df['LapStartTime'], errors='coerce').dt.total_seconds()

df = pd.merge_asof(
df.sort_values('LapStartTime_s'),
weather[['Time','AirTemp','Humidity','TrackTemp']].sort_values('Time'),
left_on='LapStartTime_s',
right_on='Time',
direction='backward'
)

df['RacePhase'] = pd.qcut(df['LapNumber'], 3,
labels=['Early','Mid','Late'])

df['GridGroup'] = pd.cut(df['GridPosition'], [0,5,10,15,20],
labels=['Top5','Top10','Midfield','Backmarker'])

df['SpeedCategory'] = pd.qcut(df['AvgSpeed'], 4, duplicates='drop')
df['SpeedCategory'] = df['SpeedCategory'].cat.rename_categories(['Slow','Medium','Fast','VeryFast'][:df['SpeedCategory'].nunique()])
